Model before rewriting:

letting g be mset(1,2)
find x: mset (maxSize 4, maxOccur(2)) of int(1..2)

such that

g = x

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])

new variables:
  find x#packed_1: int(0..8)
new constraints:
  and([sum([(x#packed_1 / 1) % 3,(x#packed_1 / 3) % 3;int(1..)]) >= 0,sum([(x#packed_1 / 1) % 3,(x#packed_1 / 3) % 3;int(1..)]) <= 4;int(1..)])
